P1. Deep events under Mount St Helens

Catalog v5.1 location review, built 2026-11-25 16:08 UTC. Requested by the PI on 2026-11-25 (07:08 PST): waveform moveouts and the information needed to judge, for each event, whether it is an earthquake and whether its v5.1 location is plausible. This page documents; it changes nothing in the catalog. Answers are kept in this browser (localStorage) and leave it only through the CSV download.

The problem in numbers

  • Box 46.05-46.30°N, 122.35-122.10°W (the faults piece's box): 7,471 located events; 331 deeper than 30 km (30-118 km), tiers A 1, B 3, C 118, D 209; median sig_z 16.6 km; median nearest station 4.5 km.
  • 22 of the deep events fall on Wednesdays 19:14-19:20 UTC, 0 on Tuesdays at that time (24 box events of any depth in the Wednesday window). Day of week of the deep events (Mon..Sun, UTC): 41, 55, 107, 35, 31, 40, 22.
  • Tier C labels among the deep events: 118, of which 33 have sig_z below 14 km with a station within 4 km.
  • The faults piece removed surface v1x_06 (39 events at about 60 km under the volcano; 64 % weekday daytime). Surface v1_ON028 holds 42 events at 23-38 km under the volcano.

Continuous data on Wednesdays 19:05-19:30 UTC against the Tuesday before

Weekly scan of every Tuesday and Wednesday 2010-2016 at HSR.UW, STD.UW (analog) and STD.CC (digital), 19:05-19:30 UTC: days with a quiet stretch of 10 s or more (1-s windows of the 2-15 Hz trace with a standard deviation below 10 % of the window's median), and how many of those start at 19:14-19:21 UTC. HSR.UW: Wednesdays 60 of 362 (45 at 19:14-19:21; first 2011-06-01, last 2016-12-21), Tuesdays 14 of 361; STD.CC: Wednesdays 0 of 351 (0 at 19:14-19:21; first nan, last nan), Tuesdays 0 of 357; STD.UW: Wednesdays 52 of 361 (50 at 19:14-19:21; first 2011-05-18, last 2014-03-19), Tuesdays 4 of 359. At STD.UW the stretches starting 19:14-21 UTC (50 Wednesdays of 120 with data, 2011-05-18 to 2013-08-28) last a median 24 s and start -3.1 s earlier each week on average (from 19:20.4 to 19:14.1 UTC; rms about the line 16 s). HSR.UW also has quiet stretches on Tuesdays and Wednesdays in late 2015 to 2016 at other clock times (a different pattern).

Station clock errors at MH07.XD and MG07.XD (2015-11-21 to 2016-05-02)

The CC differential-time QC (tomo/cc-dt, cc_dt/README.md) found record-header clock errors at MH07.XD (about 0.45 s) and MG07.XD (about 0.28 s) in co-located event pairs of 2015-11-21 to 2016-05-02; the re-timing cannot correct them, so the catalog picks carry them. In the v5.1 NonLinLoc residuals of the Mount St Helens box events (daily median minus the daily median of the other XD stations, days with 2 or more picks): MH07.XD offset by more than 0.25 s on 10 of 39 days (2015-12-06 to 2016-05-01), median +0.45 s on those days; MG07.XD offset by more than 0.25 s on 10 of 29 days (2016-03-20 to 2016-05-02), median -0.37 s on those days. P and S are offset together. Use by the populations of this report in that period: deep (> 30 km) box events: 2 of 331 events in the period, 1 with picks at MH07.XD or MG07.XD; v1_ON028 members: 1 of 42 events in the period, 0 with picks at MH07.XD or MG07.XD; v1x_06 members (removed surface): 0 of 39 events in the period, 0 with picks at MH07.XD or MG07.XD. Event views with such picks carry a line on it.

Event views

How to read an event view. Three hypocentres are compared in the same v5.1 travel-time grids and station terms, so differences come from the location only: v5.1 (NonLinLoc maximum-likelihood point, run b4_v51_iter1 or its deep pass b4_v51_deep; solid lines, star), v4 (catalog v4 hypocentre; dashed, square), ComCat (matched ComCat hypocentre when one exists; dotted, diamond). Observed picks are black (the D-B072 re-timed picks NonLinLoc used). Predicted arrival = origin time + grid travel time + station term. Each hypothesis' origin time is refitted in the v5.1 grids (its own origin time + weighted mean of observed minus predicted, weights 1/(pick error² + 0.2²)), so the ticks and the 'OT refit' residuals compare locations, not origin times; residuals with the catalog origin times are given too. The v5.1 point is the NonLinLoc optimum for these picks in these grids (EDT likelihood, which down-weights inconsistent arrivals), so its rms is expected to be the smallest; a hypocentre with a similar rms fits the picks about as well in this model, and the residuals at the nearest stations and the NLLoc weights say more about depth than the overall rms. The travel time of each arrival comes from the grid NonLinLoc used for it in v5.1 (fine 1-km grid, or wide 2-km grid); the grid reader reproduces NonLinLoc's own predicted times at the v5.1 point to 0.001 s or better for every event (value in the header). Depth: km below sea level, true depth (Earth flattening undone).
Figures. (1) maps, E-W depth section with the NonLinLoc scatter samples, the D-B068 solid surface and Slab2 (Hayes et al. 2018) where it exists, pick times against distance; (2) record section on the v5.1 origin-time axis, vertical left, horizontals right, stations sorted by epicentral distance, short coloured ticks = predicted P (above the trace) and S (below), black = observed pick; stations without a pick (grey label) are the nearest ones with data; (3) zoom on P (vertical) and S (horizontal, or vertical when the station has none) at the nearest picked stations. Filters: zero-phase 2-corner Butterworth band-pass applied forward and backward (acausal: ringing can appear up to about 1/flow s before a sharp onset); band chosen per event by distance and magnitude (2-15 Hz if the median displayed distance is below 40 km, 1-10 Hz below 150 km, 1-6 Hz beyond, 1-5 Hz for M ≥ 4.5; the low corner stays at 1 Hz or above because the ocean microseism dominates below it) and stated on each figure. Each trace is scaled to its own peak in the window (both horizontals of a station share one scale): amplitudes cannot be compared between rows.
Waveforms. pnwstore short reads of the records around the window, on the MiniSEED record-header time base (the D-B072 definition), never a merged day; the picker's band rule (HH, else BH, else EH). NC and BK are not in pnwstore: they are read from NCEDC FDSN (the project's route for these networks, utils/data_client.py) on NCEDC's times. No instrument response is removed.
